{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HkKKvaz5PQhTHuiddDPQws","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Phase 4 -- Adversarial Debate & Stack Rank (`/sc:adversarial`)\n2\t\n3\t**Phase Goal:** For every donor feature from Phase 1, run a structured `/sc:adversarial` debate to determine whether it should be absorbed into `/task`, then stack-rank the survivors. This is the core mechanism of the sprint. Each debate produces Position A (steelman for inclusion), Position B (steelman against), evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict via the binding rubric Net = (V x C) / K. The anti-sycophancy gate (R-RULE-04) and the invariant gate (R-RULE-05) are applied before any verdict is final.\n4\t\n5\t**Compliance Tier:** STANDARD (adversarial debate produces binding, auditable artifacts; V/C/K scoring must be reproducible).\n6\t\n7\t**Scoring rubric (binding, R-RULE-07):**\n8\t- **V (Value, 1-5)** — capability gain for `/task` if absorbed.\n9\t- **C (Complementarity, 1-5)** — fit with the F1 loop and phase-gate model. 5 = native fit at an existing extension point with no F1 changes; 3 = lives at a recipient extension point but needs a new field or hook; 1 = requires changing F1 invariants (auto-REJECT).\n10\t- **K (Cost, 1-5)** — integration + ongoing maintenance cost.\n11\t- **Net = (V x C) / K** — stack-rank descending.\n12\t- **Verdicts**: ADOPT (Net >= 5), ADAPT (3 <= Net < 5), DEFER (1.5 <= Net < 3), REJECT (Net < 1.5 OR violates any INV-NN).\n13\t\n14\t---\n15\t\n16\t### T04.01 — `/sc:adversarial` debates: tier classification & classification header emission\n17\t\n18\t**Roadmap Item IDs**: R-011\n19\t**Tier**: STANDARD\n20\t**Effort**: L\n21\t**MCP Requirements**: auggie MCP (`mcp__auggie-mcp__codebase-retrieval`, `directory_path: /config/workspace/IronClaude`) — primary for evidence re-resolution. Sequential — required (adversarial reasoning).\n22\t\n23\t**Artifacts (Intended Paths):**\n24\t- `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/debate-tier-classification.md`\n25\t- `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/debate-classification-header.md`\n26\t\n27\t**Deliverables:**\n28\t- One `debate-{feature-slug}.md` per feature: Position A, Position B, evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict (V, C, K, Net, verdict).\n29\t\n30\t**Steps:**\n31\t1. **[PLANNING]** Load `feature-tier-classification.md` and `feature-classification-header.md` (Phase 2) and `invariant-bounds.md` + `extension-point-contracts.md` (Phase 3) as the two source documents for each debate.\n32\t2. **[EXECUTION]** Invoke `/sc:adversarial` for the tier-classification feature: Position A steelmans inclusion (cites Phase 2 `file:line` evidence; shows the integration sketch — which extension point, what shape of change); Position B steelmans rejection (invariant-violation risk, duplication, maintenance/cognitive cost, at least one realistic failure mode introduced).\n33\t3. **[EXECUTION]** Run evidence-based weighing: each side answers the other's strongest point directly; unanswered points count against that side.\n34\t4. **[EXECUTION]** Score with the rubric — V, C (anchored to the extension-point Complementarity band from T03.02), K, Net = V x C / K; assign verdict.\n35\t5. **[EXECUTION]** Repeat steps 2-4 for classification header emission.\n36\t6. **[VERIFICATION]** Confirm both debate files have all four sections and a scored, auditable verdict; confirm C-band traces to `extension-point-contracts.md`.\n37\t7. **[COMPLETION]** Write both `debate-*.md` files to `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/`.\n38\t\n39\t**Acceptance Criteria:**\n40\t1. `debate-tier-classification.md` and `debate-classification-header.md` exist, each with Position A, Position B, evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict.\n41\t2. Position A cites Phase 2 `file:line` evidence and includes an integration sketch (extension point + shape of change).\n42\t3. Position B cites invariant-violation risk (if any), duplication (if any), maintenance cost, and at least one realistic failure mode.\n43\t4. The V/C/K/Net computation is shown explicitly and the C value traces to a Complementarity band in `extension-point-contracts.md` (R-RULE-07).\n44\t\n45\t**Validation:**\n46\t1. Manual check: reviewer recomputes Net = V x C / K and confirms the verdict matches the threshold.\n47\t2. Manual check: reviewer confirms each side answered the other's strongest point.\n48\t\n49\t**Dependencies**: T03.04 (Phase 3 checkpoint passed)\n50\t\n51\t---\n52\t\n53\t### T04.02 — `/sc:adversarial` debates: TFEP & per-tier flow branching\n54\t\n55\t**Roadmap Item IDs**: R-012\n56\t**Tier**: STANDARD\n57\t**Effort**: L\n58\t**MCP Requirements**: auggie MCP (`mcp__auggie-mcp__codebase-retrieval`, `directory_path: /config/workspace/IronClaude`) — primary. This is the core mechanism of the sprint. Each debate produces Position A (steelman for inclusion), Position B (steelman against), evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict via the binding rubric Net = (V x C) / K. The anti-sycophancy gate (R-RULE-04) and the invariant gate (R-RULE-05) are applied before any verdict is final.\n\n**Compliance Tier:** STANDARD (adversarial debate produces binding, auditable artifacts; V/C/K scoring must be reproducible).\n\n**Scoring rubric (binding, R-RULE-07):**\n- **V (Value, 1-5)** — capability gain for `/task` if absorbed.\n- **C (Complementarity, 1-5)** — fit with the F1 loop and phase-gate model. 5 = native fit at an existing extension point with no F1 changes; 3 = lives at a recipient extension point but needs a new field or hook; 1 = requires changing F1 invariants (auto-REJECT).\n- **K (Cost, 1-5)** — integration + ongoing maintenance cost.\n- **Net = (V x C) / K** — stack-rank descending.\n- **Verdicts**: ADOPT (Net >= 5), ADAPT (3 <= Net < 5), DEFER (1.5 <= Net < 3), REJECT (Net < 1.5 OR violates any INV-NN).\n\n---\n\n### T04.01 — `/sc:adversarial` debates: tier classification & classification header emission\n\n**Roadmap Item IDs**: R-011\n**Tier**: STANDARD\n**Effort**: L\n**MCP Requirements**: auggie MCP (`mcp__auggie-mcp__codebase-retrieval`, `directory_path: /config/workspace/IronClaude`) — primary for evidence re-resolution. Sequential — required (adversarial reasoning).\n\n**Artifacts (Intended Paths):**\n- `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/debate-tier-classification.md`\n- `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/debate-classification-header.md`\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- One `debate-{feature-slug}.md` per feature: Position A, Position B, evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict (V, C, K, Net, verdict).\n\n**Steps:**\n1. **[PLANNING]** Load `feature-tier-classification.md` and `feature-classification-header.md` (Phase 2) and `invariant-bounds.md` + `extension-point-contracts.md` (Phase 3) as the two source documents for each debate.\n2. **[EXECUTION]** Invoke `/sc:adversarial` for the tier-classification feature: Position A steelmans inclusion (cites Phase 2 `file:line` evidence; shows the integration sketch — which extension point, what shape of change); Position B steelmans rejection (invariant-violation risk, duplication, maintenance/cognitive cost, at least one realistic failure mode introduced).\n3. **[EXECUTION]** Run evidence-based weighing: each side answers the other's strongest point directly; unanswered points count against that side.\n4. **[EXECUTION]** Score with the rubric — V, C (anchored to the extension-point Complementarity band from T03.02), K, Net = V x C / K; assign verdict.\n5. **[EXECUTION]** Repeat steps 2-4 for classification header emission.\n6. **[VERIFICATION]** Confirm both debate files have all four sections and a scored, auditable verdict; confirm C-band traces to `extension-point-contracts.md`.\n7. **[COMPLETION]** Write both `debate-*.md` files to `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/`.\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n1. `debate-tier-classification.md` and `debate-classification-header.md` exist, each with Position A, Position B, evidence-based weighing, and a scored verdict.\n2. Position B cites invariant-violation risk (if any), duplication (if any), maintenance cost, and at least one realistic failure mode.\n4. The V/C/K/Net computation is shown explicitly and the C value traces to a Complementarity band in `extension-point-contracts.md` (R-RULE-07).\n\n**Validation:**\n1. Telemetry\nEXT_SEEN=false\n[ \"$REASON\" = \"external_change\" ] && EXT_SEEN=true\n{\n    jq -nc \\\n        --arg ts \"$NOW_ISO\" --arg tool \"$TOOL_NAME\" --arg path \"$TARGET\" \\\n        --arg sid \"$SESSION_ID\" --argjson idx \"$TC_IDX\" \\\n        --arg age \"$READ_AGE\" --argjson ext \"$EXT_SEEN\" \\\n        --arg dec \"$DECISION\" --arg rsn \"$REASON\" \\\n        '{ts:$ts, event:\"PreToolUse\", tool:$tool, path:$path, session_id:$sid, tool_call_idx:$idx, recent_read_age_sec:($age|if . == \"null\" then null else (tonumber? // null) end), external_change_seen:$ext, decision:$dec, reason:$rsn}' \\\n        2>/dev/null\n} >> \"$TELEMETRY\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# 10. Block or allow\nif [ \"$DECISION\" = \"block\" ]; then\n    case \"$REASON\" in\n        no_prior_read)\n            echo \"You have not Read \\`$TARGET\\` in this session. Read it before editing.\" >&2 ;;\n        read_too_old)\n            echo \"You last Read \\`$TARGET\\` ${READ_AGE}s ago, beyond the 30-minute freshness horizon. Re-Read before editing.\" >&2 ;;\n        external_change)\n            echo \"\\`$TARGET\\` was modified after your last Read (mtime change detected). Re-Read before editing.\" >&2 ;;\n        *)\n            echo \"Re-Read \\`$TARGET\\` before editing (freshness gate: $REASON).\" >&2 ;;\n    esac\n    exit 2\nfi\n\nexit 0","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
